Choice text. -> TargetScene\n2. [Skill] Choice with skill check. -> TargetScene\n```\n\n## Key Parsing Elements\n\n### Scene Blocks\n- Denoted by `[SceneID]` on a line\n- Contains one or more lines until the next scene block\n- Scene ID becomes the node ID\n\n### Line Types\n\n**Narrative/Dialogue Lines:**\n- Format: `Speaker: Text -> NextScene`\n- Speaker followed by colon, then text\n- Arrow points to single next scene\n- Type: \"line\"\n\n**Choice Lines:**\n- Format: `N. [Skill] Text -> NextScene`\n- Numbered (1-N)\n- Optional skill in brackets (e.g., `[Intimidate]`, `[Lie]`)\n- Arrow points to target scene\n- Type: \"choice\"\n\n### Parsing Strategy\n\n1. Split input by blank lines to identify scene blocks\n2. Extract scene ID from `[ID]` pattern\n3. For each line in scene:\n   - Parse speaker (if present) using `:` delimiter\n   - Extract destination using `->` pattern\n   - Determine type (line or choice)\n   - Create nodes and edges\n\n### Special Cases\n\n- **Multiple lines per scene**: Each line is a separate node\n- **Empty destinations**: Last scenes with no arrow\n- **Circular references**: Loop back to previous scenes\n- **End scenes**: Typically named \"End\", multiple paths can converge\n\n## Implementation Notes\n\n- Use regex for robust parsing: `r'(\\w+):\\s*(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\w+)'` for lines\n- Use regex for choices: `r'^(\\d+)\\.\\s*(?:\\[([^\\]]+)\\])?\\s*(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\w+)'`\n- Strip whitespace from all extracted values\n- Handle edge case of last node with no destination\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":10584,"cache_read_input_tokens":19338,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":10584,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"9d776c75-b51e-4c65-8b7c-47847c5b491c","uuid":"4fbc0840-3ae5-42e5-8cfe-c49c6fef7769"}

JSON Graph Format for Dialogues\n\n## Standard Format\n\n```json\n{\n  \"nodes\": [\n    {\n      \"id\": \"NodeID\",\n      \"text\": \"The dialogue or choice text\",\n      \"speaker\": \"Character name or 'Narrator'\",\n      \"type\": \"line|choice\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"edges\": [\n    {\n      \"from\": \"SourceNodeID\",\n      \"to\": \"TargetNodeID\",\n      \"text\": \"Edge label or choice text\"\n    }\n  ]\n}\n```\n\n## Node Properties\n\n| Property | Type | Description |\n|----------|------|-------------|\n| id | string | Unique identifier (alphanumeric, no spaces) |\n| text | string | The dialogue/choice content |\n| speaker | string | Who is speaking (\"Narrator\", character name, or scene ID for choices) |\n| type | string | Either \"line\" (dialogue) or \"choice\" (numbered option) |\n\n## Edge Properties\n\n| Property | Type | Description |\n|----------|------|-------------|\n| from | string | Source node ID |\n| to | string | Target node ID |\n| text | string | Label (often the choice text for choice edges) |\n\n## Graph Constraints\n\n1. **Reachability**: All nodes must be reachable from the first node\n2. **Valid Targets**: All edge targets must exist (except for \"End\")\n3. **Convergence**: Multiple edges can target the same node (fan-in)\n4. **Divergence**: Multiple edges from same node (fan-out) represents branching\n\n## Serialization Tips\n\n- Use `json.dumps(graph, indent=2)` for readable output\n- Always validate structure before writing\n- Sort nodes by appearance order for consistency\n- Preserve exact text from original script\n\n## Validation Checklist\n\n- [ ] All nodes have unique IDs\n- [ ] All \"to\" references in edges exist as node IDs (except \"End\")\n- [ ] No circular edge definitions (graphs should be DAG)\n- [ ] Speaker field populated for all nodes\n- [ ] Text field not empty for all Graphviz DOT Format for Dialogue Visualization\n\n## Overview\nDOT language is Graphviz's standard format for representing directed graphs visually.\n\n## Basic Syntax\n\n```dot\ndigraph DialogueGraph {\n  graph [rankdir=LR, splines=ortho];\n  node [shape=box, style=rounded];\n\n  NodeA [label=\"Text\"];\n  NodeB [label=\"Text\"];\n\n  NodeA -> NodeB [label=\"transition\"];\n}\n```\n\n## Graph-Level Attributes\n\n| Attribute | Purpose | Examples |\n|-----------|---------|----------|\n| rankdir | Direction (LR=left-right, TD=top-down) | `rankdir=LR` |\n| splines | Edge routing appearance | box, ellipse, diamond, plaintext |\n| style | Visual style | filled, rounded, bold |\n| fillcolor | Fill color | white, lightblue, #FFFFFF |\n| label | Display text | Any string (use End [label=\"End\", shape=ellipse, fillcolor=lightgreen];\n\n  Start -> Choice1 [label=\"transition\"];\n  Choice1 -> End [label=\"any path\"];\n}\n```\n\n## Implementation Tips\n\n1. **Escape special Reachability Check\nEnsure all nodes are reachable from the starting node using BFS/DFS.\n\n```python\ndef is_reachable_from_start(graph, start_id=\"Start\"):\n    \"\"\"Check if all nodes are reachable from start node.\"\"\"\n    visited = set()\n    queue = [start_id]\n    edges_by_source = {e['from']: [] for e in graph['edges']}\n    for e in graph['edges']:\n        edges_by_source[e['from']].append(e['to'])\n\n    while queue:\n        node = queue.pop(0)\n        if node in visited:\n            continue\n        visited.add(node)\n        queue.extend(edges_by_source.get(node, []))\n\n    node_ids = {n['id'] for n in graph['nodes']}\n    unreachable = node_ids - visited\n    return len(unreachable) == 0, unreachable\n```\n\n### 2. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_01TgfCe422BcbncV1zLELrzu","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01TVD6QJaa5LnwRgDtuVTVPc","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/app/solution.py","content":"import re\nimport json\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Any, Set, Tuple\n\n\ndef parse_script(text: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Parse a dialogue script into a structured graph.\n\n    Args:\n        text: The script content as a string\n\n    Returns:\n        A dictionary with 'nodes' and 'edges' keys representing the dialogue graph\n    \"\"\"\n    nodes: List[Dict[str, str]] = []\n    edges: List[Dict[str, str]] = []\n    node_id_counter = {}  # Track node IDs within each scene\n\n    # Split by scene blocks\n    scene_pattern = r'\\[(\\w+)\\]'\n    parts = re.split(scene_pattern, text.strip())\n\n    # parts[0] is empty or pre-scene content\n    # Then alternating: [scene_id, scene_content, scene_id, scene_content, ...]\n    scenes = {}\n    for i in range(1, len(parts), 2):\n        if i + 1 < len(parts):\n            scene_id = parts[i]\n            scene_content = parts[i + 1]\n            scenes[scene_id] = scene_content\n\n    # Track first scene for later use\n    first_scene = None\n\n    # Process each scene\n    for scene_id, content in scenes.items():\n        if first_scene is None:\n            first_scene = scene_id\n\n        lines = content.strip().split('\\n')\n        line_number = 0\n\n        for line in lines:\n            line = line.strip()\n            if not line:\n                continue\n\n            # Check if it's a choice line (starts with number)\n            choice_match = re.match(r'^(\\d+)\\.\\s*(?:\\[([^\\]]+)\\])?\\s*(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\w+)\\s*$', line)\n            if choice_match:\n                choice_num = choice_match.group(1)\n                skill = choice_match.group(2) or \"\"\n                text_content = choice_match.group(3)\n                destination = choice_match.group(4)\n\n                # Create unique node ID for this choice\n                node_id = f\"{scene_id}_choice_{choice_num}\"\n\n                # Build choice text with skill if present\n                if skill:\n                    full_text = f\"[{skill}] {text_content}\"\n                else:\n                    full_text = text_content\n\n                nodes.append({\n                    \"id\": node_id,\n                    \"text\": full_text,\n                    \"speaker\": scene_id,\n                    \"type\": \"choice\"\n                })\n\n                edges.append({\n                    \"from\": node_id,\n                    \"to\": destination,\n                    \"text\": full_text\n                })\n                continue\n\n            # Check if it's a dialogue line (Speaker: Text -> Destination)\n            dialogue_match = re.match(r'^([^:]+):\\s*(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\w+)\\s*$', line)\n            if dialogue_match:\n                speaker = dialogue_match.group(1).strip()\n                text_content = dialogue_match.group(2).strip()\n                destination = dialogue_match.group(3).strip()\n\n                # Create unique node ID for this line\n                if scene_id not in node_id_counter:\n                    node_id_counter[scene_id] = 0\n                node_id_counter[scene_id] += 1\n                node_id = f\"{scene_id}_line_{node_id_counter[scene_id]}\"\n\n                nodes.append({\n                    \"id\": node_id,\n                    \"text\": text_content,\n                    \"speaker\": speaker,\n                    \"type\": \"line\"\n                })\n\n                edges.append({\n                    \"from\": node_id,\n                    \"to\": destination,\n                    \"text\": \"\"\n                })\n                continue\n\n            # Check if it's a dialogue line without destination (dead end)\n            simple_dialogue_match = re.match(r'^([^:]+):\\s*(.+?)$', line)\n            if simple_dialogue_match:\n                speaker = simple_dialogue_match.group(1).strip()\n                text_content = simple_dialogue_match.group(2).strip()\n\n                if scene_id not in node_id_counter:\n                    node_id_counter[scene_id] = 0\n                node_id_counter[scene_id] += 1\n                node_id = f\"{scene_id}_line_{node_id_counter[scene_id]}\"\n\n                nodes.append({\n                    \"id\": node_id,\n                    \"text\": text_content,\n                    \"speaker\": speaker,\n                    \"type\": \"line\"\n                })\n                continue\n\n    # Build the graph structure with first node reference\n    graph = {\n        \"nodes\": nodes,\n        \"edges\": edges,\n        \"start_node\": f\"{first_scene}_line_1\" if first_scene else None\n    }\n\n    return graph\n\n\ndef validate_graph(graph: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[bool, List[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Validate the graph structure.\n\n    Args:\n        graph: The graph dictionary with nodes and edges\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (is_valid, error_messages)\n    \"\"\"\n    errors = []\n\n    # Check 1: Node uniqueness\n    node_ids = [n['id'] for n in graph['nodes']]\n    if len(node_ids) != len(set(node_ids)):\n        errors.append(\"Duplicate node IDs found\")\n\n    # Check 2: All edge targets must exist (except End)\n    valid_ids = {n['id'] for n in graph['nodes']} | {'End'}\n    for edge in graph['edges']:\n        if edge['to'] not in valid_ids:\n            errors.append(f\"Edge target '{edge['to']}' does not exist as a node\")\n\n    # Check 3: Reachability from first node\n    node_ids_set = {n['id'] for n in graph['nodes']}\n\n    # Find start node (first node with lowest ID or explicitly marked)\n    start_node = graph.get('start_node')\n    if start_node and start_node in node_ids_set:\n        visited = _reachability_check(graph, start_node)\n        unreachable = node_ids_set - visited\n        if unreachable:\n            errors.append(f\"Unreachable nodes: {unreachable}\")\n\n    return len(errors) == 0, errors\n\n\ndef _reachability_check(graph: Dict[str, Any], start_node: str) -> Set[str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Check which nodes are reachable from the start node using BFS.\n\n    Args:\n        graph: The graph dictionary\n        start_node: Starting node ID\n\n    Returns:\n        Set of reachable node IDs\n    \"\"\"\n    visited = set()\n    queue = [start_node]\n\n    # Build adjacency list\n    adjacency = {}\n    for edge in graph['edges']:\n        if edge['from'] not in adjacency:\n            adjacency[edge['from']] = []\n        adjacency[edge['from']].append(edge['to'])\n\n    while queue:\n        node = queue.pop(0)\n        if node in visited:\n            continue\n        visited.add(node)\n\n        for neighbor in adjacency.get(node, []):\n            if neighbor not in visited and neighbor != 'End':  # End is always reachable\n                queue.append(neighbor)\n\n    return visited\n\n\ndef generate_dot(graph: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Generate Graphviz DOT format visualization.\n\n    Args:\n        graph: The graph dictionary\n\n    Returns:\n        DOT format string\n    \"\"\"\n    lines = [\n        'digraph DialogueGraph {',\n        '  rankdir=LR;',\n        '  graph [bgcolor=white];',\n        '  node [shape=box, style=\"rounded,filled\", fillcolor=lightblue];',\n        ''\n    ]\n\n    # Track node IDs within each scene\n\n    # Split by scene blocks\n    scene_pattern = r'\\[(\\w+)\\]'\n    parts = re.split(scene_pattern, text.strip())\n\n    # parts[0] is empty or pre-scene content\n    # Track node IDs within each scene\n\n    # Split by scene blocks\n    scene_pattern = r'\\[(\\w+)\\]'\n    parts = re.split(scene_pattern, text.strip())\n\n    # parts[0] is empty or pre-scene content\n    # Then alternating: [scene_id, scene_content, scene_id, scene_content, ...]\n    scenes = {}\n    for i in range(1, len(parts), 2):\n        if i + 1 < len(parts):\n            scene_id = parts[i]\n            scene_content = parts[i + 1]\n            scenes[scene_id] = scene_content\n\n    # Check if it's a choice line (starts with number)\n            choice_match = re.match(r'^(\\d+)\\.\\s*(?:\\[([^\\]]+)\\])?\\s*(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\w+)\\s*$', line)\n            if choice_match:\n                choice_num = choice_match.group(1)\n                skill = choice_match.group(2) or \"\"\n                text_content = choice_match.group(3)\n                destination = choice_match.group(4)\n\n                # Create unique node ID for this choice\n                node_id = f\"{scene_id}_choice_{choice_num}\"\n\n                # Build choice text with skill if present\n                if skill:\n                    full_text = f\"[{skill}] {text_content}\"\n                else:\n                    full_text = text_content\n\n                nodes.append({\n                    \"id\": node_id,\n                    \"text\": full_text,\n                    \"speaker\": scene_id,\n                    \"type\": \"choice\"\n                })\n\n                edges.append({\n                    \"from\": node_id,\n                    \"to\": destination,\n                    \"text\": full_text\n                })\n                continue\n\n            # Check if it's a dialogue line without destination (dead end)\n            simple_dialogue_match = re.match(r'^([^:]+):\\s*(.+?)$', line)\n            if simple_dialogue_match:\n                speaker = simple_dialogue_match.group(1).strip()\n                text_content = simple_dialogue_match.group(2).strip()\n\n                if scene_id not in node_id_counter:\n                    node_id_counter[scene_id] = 0\n                node_id_counter[scene_id] += 1\n                node_id = f\"{scene_id}_line_{node_id_counter[scene_id]}\"\n\n                nodes.append({\n                    \"id\": node_id,\n                    \"text\": text_content,\n                    \"speaker\": speaker,\n                    \"type\": \"line\"\n                })\n                continue\n\n    # Split by scene blocks\n    scene_pattern = r'\\[(\\w+)\\]'\n    parts = re.split(scene_pattern, text.strip())\n\n    # parts[0] is empty or pre-scene content\n    # Then alternating: [scene_id, scene_content, scene_id, scene_content, ...]\n    scenes = {}\n    scene_order = []  # Track order of scenes\n    for i in range(1, len(parts), 2):\n        if i + 1 < len(parts):\n            scene_id = parts[i]\n            scene_content = parts[i + 1]\n            scenes[scene_id] = scene_content\n            scene_order.append(scene_id)\n\n    # Track first scene for later use\n    first_scene = scene_order[0] if scene_order else None\n\n    # Process each scene\n    for scene_id in scene_order:\n        content = scenes[scene_id]\n        lines = content.strip().split('\\n')\n\n        # Collect all lines and choices in this scene\n        scene_lines = []\n        scene_choices = []\n        scene_text = []\n        speaker = None\n\n        for line in lines:\n            line = line.strip()\n            if not line:\n                continue\n\n            # Check if it's a choice line (starts with number)\n            choice_match = re.match(r'^(\\d+)\\.\\s*(?:\\[([^\\]]+)\\])?\\s*(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\w+)\\s*$', line)\n            if choice_match:\n                choice_num = choice_match.group(1)\n                skill = choice_match.group(2) or \"\"\n                text_content = choice_match.group(3)\n                destination = choice_match.group(4)\n\n                # Build choice text with skill if present\n                if skill:\n                    full_text = f\"[{skill}] {text_content}\"\n                else:\n                    full_text = text_content\n\n                scene_choices.append({\n                    \"number\": choice_num,\n                    \"text\": full_text,\n                    \"destination\": destination\n                })\n                continue\n\n            # Check if it's a dialogue line (Speaker: Text -> Destination or Speaker: Text)\n            dialogue_match = re.match(r'^([^:]+):\\s*(.+?)\\s*(?:->\\s*(\\w+))?\\s*$', line)\n            if dialogue_match:\n                speaker = dialogue_match.group(1).strip()\n                text_content = dialogue_match.group(2).strip()\n                destination = dialogue_match.group(3)\n\n                scene_lines.append({\n                    \"speaker\": speaker,\n                    \"text\": text_content,\n                    \"destination\": destination\n                })\n\n        # Create a single node per scene\n        if scene_lines or scene_choices:\n            # Determine node type and text\n            if scene_choices:\n                # Scene with choices is a choice node\n                node_type = \"choice\"\n                # Text is the scene narrative plus choice options\n                text_parts = [line[\"text\"] for line in scene_lines]\n                choice_parts = [f\"{c['number']}. {c['text']}\" for c in scene_choices]\n                full_text = \"\\n\".join(text_parts + choice_parts)\n                node_speaker = scene_lines[0][\"speaker\"] if scene_lines else scene_id\n            else:\n                # Scene with only dialogue is a line node\n                node_type = \"line\"\n                full_text = \"\\n\".join([line[\"text\"] for line in scene_lines])\n                node_speaker = scene_lines[0][\"speaker\"] if scene_lines else scene_id\n\n            nodes.append({\n                \"id\": scene_id,\n                \"text\": full_text,\n                \"speaker\": node_speaker,\n                \"type\": node_type\n            })\n\n            # Check 3: Reachability from first node\n    node_ids_set = {n['id'] for n in graph['nodes']}\n\n    # Find start node (first node with lowest ID or explicitly marked)\n    start_node = graph.get('start_node')\n    if start_node and start_node in node_ids_set:\n        visited = _reachability_check(graph, start_node)\n        unreachable = node_ids_set - visited\n        if unreachable:\n            errors.append(f\"Unreachable nodes: {unreachable}\")\n\n    return len(errors) == 0, errors\n\n\ndef _reachability_check(graph: Dict[str, Any], start_node: str) -> Set[str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Check which nodes are reachable from the start node using BFS.\n\n    Args:\n        graph: The graph dictionary\n        start_node: Starting node ID\n\n    Returns:\n        Set of reachable node IDs\n    \"\"\"\n    visited = set()\n    queue = [start_node]\n\n    # Build adjacency list\n    adjacency = {}\n    for edge in graph['edges']:\n        if edge['from'] not in adjacency:\n            adjacency[edge['from']] = []\n        adjacency[edge['from']].append(edge['to'])\n\n    while queue:\n        node = queue.pop(0)\n        if node in visited:\n            continue\n        visited.add(node)\n\n        for neighbor in adjacency.get(node, []):\n            if neighbor not in visited and neighbor != 'End':  # End is always reachable\n                queue.append(neighbor)\n\n    return visited\n\n\ndef generate_dot(graph: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Generate Graphviz DOT format visualization.\n\n    Args:\n        graph: The graph dictionary\n\n    Returns:\n        DOT format string\n    \"\"\"\n    lines = [\n        'digraph DialogueGraph {',\n        '  rankdir=LR;',\n        '  graph [bgcolor=white];',\n        '  node [shape=box, style=\"rounded,filled\", fillcolor=lightblue];',\n        ''\n    ]\n\n    # Define node styles by type\n    for node in graph['nodes']:\n        node_id = node['id']\n        label = node['text'].replace('\"', '\\\\\"')\n\n        # Limit label length and add newlines\n        if len(label) > 30:\n            words = label.split()\n            label_lines = []\n            current_line = []\n            for word in words:\n                current_line.append(word)\n                if len(' '.join(current_line)) > 25:\n                    label_lines.append(' '.join(current_line[:-1]))\n                    current_line = [word]\n            if current_line:\n                label_lines.append(' '.join(current_line))\n            label = '\\\\n'.join(label_lines)\n\n        if node['type'] == 'choice':\n            lines.append(f'  {node_id} [label=\"{label}\", shape=diamond, fillcolor=lightyellow];')\n        else:\n            lines.append(f'  {node_id} [label=\"{label}\", fillcolor=lightblue];')\n\n    # Add End node if referenced\n    has_end = any(e['to'] == 'End' for e in graph['edges'])\n    if has_end:\n        lines.append('  End [label=\"End\", shape=ellipse, fillcolor=lightgreen];')\n\n    lines.append('')\n\n    # Add edges\n    for edge in graph['edges']:\n        from_node = edge['from']\n        to_node = edge['to']\n        lines.append(f'  {from_node} -> {to_node};')\n\n    lines.append('}')\n\n    return '\\n'.join(lines)\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    # Read the script file\n    with open('/app/script.txt', 'r') as f:\n        script_content = f.read()\n\n    # Parse the script\n    graph = parse_script(script_content)\n\n    # Validate the graph\n    is_valid, errors = validate_graph(graph)\n    if not is_valid:\n        print(\"Validation errors found:\")\n        for error in errors:\n            print(f\"  - {error}\")\n\n    # Split by scene blocks\n    scene_pattern = r'\\[(\\w+)\\]'\n    parts = re.split(scene_pattern, text.strip())\n\n    # parts[0] is empty or pre-scene content\n    # Then alternating: [scene_id, scene_content, scene_id, scene_content, ...]\n    scenes = {}\n    scene_order = []  # Track order of scenes\n    for i in range(1, len(parts), 2):\n        if i + 1 < len(parts):\n            scene_id = parts[i]\n            scene_content = parts[i + 1]\n            scenes[scene_id] = scene_content\n            scene_order.append(scene_id)\n\n    # Track first scene for later use\n    first_scene = scene_order[0] if scene_order else None\n\n    # Process each scene\n    for scene_id in scene_order:\n        content = scenes[scene_id]\n        lines = content.strip().split('\\n')\n\n        # Collect all lines and choices in this scene\n        scene_lines = []\n        scene_choices = []\n        scene_text = []\n        speaker = None\n\n        for line in lines:\n            line = line.strip()\n            if not line:\n                continue\n\n            # Check if it's a choice line (starts with number)\n            choice_match = re.match(r'^(\\d+)\\.\\s*(?:\\[([^\\]]+)\\])?\\s*(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\w+)\\s*$', line)\n            if choice_match:\n                choice_num = choice_match.group(1)\n                skill = choice_match.group(2) or \"\"\n                text_content = choice_match.group(3)\n                destination = choice_match.group(4)\n\n                # Build choice text with skill if present\n                if skill:\n                    full_text = f\"[{skill}] {text_content}\"\n                else:\n                    full_text = text_content\n\n                scene_choices.append({\n                    \"number\": choice_num,\n                    \"text\": full_text,\n                    \"destination\": destination\n                })\n                continue\n\n            # Check if it's a dialogue line (Speaker: Text -> Destination or Speaker: Text)\n            dialogue_match = re.match(r'^([^:]+):\\s*(.+?)\\s*(?:->\\s*(\\w+))?\\s*$', line)\n            if dialogue_match:\n                speaker = dialogue_match.group(1).strip()\n                text_content = dialogue_match.group(2).strip()\n                destination = dialogue_match.group(3)\n\n                scene_lines.append({\n                    \"speaker\": speaker,\n                    \"text\": text_content,\n                    \"destination\": destination\n                })\n\n        # Create a single node per scene\n        if scene_lines or scene_choices:\n            # Determine node type and text\n            if scene_choices:\n                # Scene with choices is a choice node\n                node_type = \"choice\"\n                # Scene with only dialogue is a line node\n                node_type = \"line\"\n                full_text = \"\\n\".join([line[\"text\"] for line in scene_lines])\n                node_speaker = scene_lines[0][\"speaker\"] if scene_lines else scene_id\n\n            nodes.append({\n                \"id\": scene_id,\n                \"text\": full_text,\n                \"speaker\": node_speaker,\n                \"type\": node_type\n            })\n\n            # Create edges from this scene\n            # First, edges from dialogue lines with destinations\n            for line in scene_lines:\n                if line[\"destination\"]:\n                    edges.append({\n                        \"from\": scene_id,\n                        \"to\": line[\"destination\"],\n                        \"text\": \"\"\n                    })\n                    break  # Only take the first dialogue destination\n\n            # Then, edges from choices\n            for choice in scene_choices:\n                edges.append({\n                    \"from\": scene_id,\n                    \"to\": choice[\"destination\"],\n                    \"text\": choice[\"text\"]\n                })\n\n    # Check 1: Node uniqueness\n    node_ids = [n['id'] for n in graph['nodes']]\n    if len(node_ids) != len(set(node_ids)):\n        errors.append(\"Duplicate node IDs found\")\n\n    # Check 2: All edge targets must exist (except End)\n    valid_ids = {n['id'] for n in graph['nodes']} | {'End'}\n    for edge in graph['edges']:\n        if edge['to'] not in valid_ids:\n            errors.append(f\"Edge target '{edge['to']}' does not exist as a node\")\n\n    # Check 3: Reachability from first node\n    node_ids_set = {n['id'] for n in graph['nodes']}\n\n    # Find start node (first node with lowest ID or explicitly marked)\n    start_node = graph.get('start_node')\n    if start_node and start_node in node_ids_set:\n        visited = _reachability_check(graph, start_node)\n        unreachable = node_ids_set - visited\n        if unreachable:\n            errors.append(f\"Unreachable nodes: {unreachable}\")\n\n    return len(errors) == 0, errors\n\n\ndef _reachability_check(graph: Dict[str, Any], start_node: str) -> Set[str]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Check which nodes are reachable from the start node using BFS.\n\n    Args:\n        graph: The graph dictionary\n        start_node: Starting node ID\n\n    Returns:\n        Set of reachable node IDs\n    \"\"\"\n    visited = set()\n    queue = [start_node]\n\n    # Build adjacency list\n    adjacency = {}\n    for edge in graph['edges']:\n        if edge['from'] not in adjacency:\n            adjacency[edge['from']] = []\n        adjacency[edge['from']].append(edge['to'])\n\n    while queue:\n        node = queue.pop(0)\n        if node in visited:\n            continue\n        visited.add(node)\n\n        for neighbor in adjacency.get(node, []):\n            if neighbor not in visited and neighbor != 'End':  # End is always reachable\n                queue.append(neighbor)\n\n    return visited\n\n\ndef generate_dot(graph: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Generate Graphviz DOT format visualization.\n\n    Args:\n        graph: The graph dictionary\n\n    Returns:\n        DOT format string\n    \"\"\"\n    lines = [\n        'digraph DialogueGraph {',\n        '  rankdir=LR;',\n        '  graph [bgcolor=white];',\n        '  node [shape=box, style=\"rounded,filled\", fillcolor=lightblue];',\n        ''\n    ]\n\n    # Define node styles by type\n    for node in graph['nodes']:\n        node_id = node['id']\n        label = node['text'].replace('\"', '\\\\\"')\n\n        # Limit label length and add newlines\n        if len(label) > 30:\n            words = label.split()\n            label_lines = []\n            current_line = []\n            for word in words:\n                current_line.append(word)\n                if len(' '.join(current_line)) > 25:\n                    label_lines.append(' '.join(current_line[:-1]))\n                    current_line = [word]\n            if current_line:\n                label_lines.append(' '.join(current_line))\n            label = '\\\\n'.join(label_lines)\n\n        if node['type'] == 'choice':\n            lines.append(f'  {node_id} [label=\"{label}\", shape=diamond, fillcolor=lightyellow];')\n        else:\n            lines.append(f'  {node_id} [label=\"{label}\", fillcolor=lightblue];')\n\n    # Add End node if referenced\n    has_end = any(e['to'] == 'End' for e in graph['edges'])\n    if has_end:\n        lines.append('  End [label=\"End\", shape=ellipse, fillcolor=lightgreen];')\n\n    lines.append('')\n\n    # Add edges\n    for edge in graph['edges']:\n        from_node = edge['from']\n        to_node = edge['to']\n        lines.append(f'  {from_node} -> {to_node};')\n\n    lines.append('}')\n\n    return '\\n'.join(lines)\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    # Read the script file\n    with open('/app/script.txt', 'r') as f:\n        script_content = f.read()\n\n    # Parse the script\n    graph = parse_script(script_content)\n\n    # Validate the graph\n    is_valid, errors = validate_graph(graph)\n    if not is_valid:\n        print(\"Validation errors found:\")\n        for error in errors:\n            print(f\"  - {error}\")\n\n    # Write JSON output\n    with open('/app/dialogue.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump({\"nodes\": graph['nodes'], \"edges\": graph['edges']}, f, indent=2)\n\n    # Generate and write DOT output\n    dot_content = generate_dot(graph)\n    with open('/app/dialogue.dot', 'w') as f:\n        f.write(dot_content)\n\n    print(f\"Parsed {len(graph['nodes'])} nodes and {len(graph['edges'])} edges\")\n    print(f\"Graph valid: {is_valid}\")\n    print(f\"Output written to /app/dialogue.json and /app/dialogue.dot\")\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":16,"oldLines":10,"newStart":16,"newLines":38,"lines":["     nodes: List[Dict[str, str]] = []","     edges: List[Dict[str, str]] = []"," ","-    #
